# CLAUDE.md

Context for new Claude Code sessions in this repo. **Read this first, then update it whenever you push changes.**

## What this repo is

A Python 3.10+ Claude-driven Solana memecoin scanner/trader. Paper trading is the default. It was built as a better version of the "Grok bots" people promote online. `RESEARCH.md` holds the findings: the viral returns are survivorship bias, and the realistic edge comes from risk control plus measurement.

**Naming (user, 11-05):** every quant (systematic trading) strategy belongs to **MZFq**: Supermeme (this bot's books) and MZF Short Only (`shortbook/`) are MZFq programs. Lending strategies (on-chain venture debt, the `mzf-protocol` repo) are **MZF Protocol**. Use these names in docs, reports and the dashboard.

## Layout

| Path | Role |
|---|---|
| `tradingbot/config.py` | `Config` dataclass; every field is overridable with a `TB_<FIELD>` env var or `with_overrides({"field": "text"})`. Live mode needs `TB_LIVE_CONFIRM=I_ACCEPT_TOTAL_LOSS_RISK` |
| `tradingbot/models.py` | `PairSnapshot`, `SafetyReport`, `FactorResult`, `Verdict` (pydantic), `Position` |
| `tradingbot/data/dexscreener.py` | Discovery feeds (profiles, boosts) + `/tokens/v1/solana/{csv}` pair data. Pump.fun bonding-curve pairs have **no liquidity field** |
| `tradingbot/data/geckoterminal.py` | All calls go through `_get` with one process-wide pace (`MIN_INTERVAL_S`=2.2s, free API ~30/min per IP; tests pass `min_interval_s=0`) and one 429 retry. Watcher endpoints: `new_pools(page)`, `pools(addresses)` (multi, 30 per call), `token_info(mint)`, `ohlcv(pool)`; helpers `base_token`, `dex_id`. `GeckoTerminal.discover()`: base-token mints of `/networks/solana/trending_pools` for 1h and 6h (deduped, SOL/USDC/USDT skipped). Only addresses come from here; snapshots still come from DexScreener. `Engine.discover()` merges feeds per `cfg.discovery`="dexscreener,geckoterminal" and records `PairSnapshot.sources` (shown to Claude as `discovered_via`, stored in decision snapshots) |
| `tradingbot/data/rugcheck.py` | `api.rugcheck.xyz/v1/tokens/{mint}/report/summary`. `None` result = unsafe |
| `tradingbot/factors.py` | `market_filters` -> `safety_filters` -> weighted 0..1 composite (`WEIGHTS`). No LLM here |
| `tradingbot/analyst.py` | Claude call: `claude-opus-5-5`, adaptive thinking, effort `medium`, `web_search_20260209`, JSON-schema `output_config`, server-side refusal fallback (`fallbacks="default"`, beta `server-side-fallback-2026-07-01`), `pause_turn` resume loop, hourly call cap. `last_outcome` = `ok` / `failed` (token-specific: engine cools the token) / `unavailable` (billing or auth error pauses `api_account_pause_s`=600; 429, 5xx or network pauses `api_transient_pause_s`=60; the token stays queued and the failed call is not counted against the cap). `is_billing_error()` matches `billing_error` type or "credit balance"/"spend limit" text |
| `tradingbot/triage.py` | Cheap first pass before the analyst: `Triage.check()` sends the same `build_brief` to `triage_model`=`claude-haiku-5-5` (effort `triage_effort`=low, JSON schema, no web search, no `fallbacks`: Haiku has no server-side fallback) and returns `{decision analyse|skip, reason, red_flags, audit}`. Skips become decisions with stage `triaged_out` (tracked, count as seen for 6h, enter the `random` baseline pool and its buy-rate denominator); `triage_audit_pct`=10% of skips still go to the full analysis (`audit: true`) to measure what triage throws away. Fails open on a bad response; stops the cycle when the API is unavailable. Built in `cli.build_engine` when `cfg.triage`; `Engine(triage=None)` by default, so older tests are unaffected. Cost kind `triage` (in the claude book's API bill). `decisions.triage` column (migration). Report: `TRIAGE` section + `triaged out` outcome group |
| `tradingbot/watcher.py` | **Pump watcher (research, never trades, no Claude).** Own thread (`watch_poll_s`=30, started in `run_forever` when `cfg.watch` and the DB is not `:memory:`), own SQLite `watch.db` next to the journal (`watch_pools`, `watch_snaps`, `watch_state`). Discovers GeckoTerminal `new_pools` (≤3 pages, stops when caught up); bonding-curve pools (`LAUNCHPAD_DEXES`: pump-fun, meteora-dbc, raydium-launchlab, bags-fm, …) kept at `watch_launch_sample_pct`=20 by address hash, all other pools (graduations) kept. Snapshots at `watch_ages_min`=5,15,30,60,180,360,1440 via `/pools/multi` (`compact()`: price, liq, fdv, buys/sells/buyers/sellers/vol/chg per m5..h24); snapshots >max(10 min, 50% of age) late are stored as NULL (missed), pools not returned as '{}'. Token info at `watch_info_age_min`=15 (`compact_info`: socials, developer wallet + holding %, graduation %, authorities, holders). After 24h: 5-min candles (`ohlcv`) if 24h volume ≥ `watch_ohlcv_min_volume_usd`=5000, then `closed=1`; asked for by end time (`before_timestamp` = created + `OHLCV_WINDOW_S`=25h, stored in `ohlcv_end`, column added by migration in `WatchStore.__init__`) so late fetches still get the first 25h; `OHLCV_CALLS_PER_STEP`=2, failures retried `OHLCV_MAX_TRIES`=3; spare calls refetch old 'latest 300' sets whose first candle is >1h after creation (`_refetch_queue`, built once per run; GeckoTerminal's 300 counts only traded periods, so only busy tokens lost their start: 36 of 1,533 on 10-19). Token info: newest first, `INFO_CALLS_PER_STEP`=4, given up `INFO_USEFUL_S`=45 min after due (later info can't feed the 15/30/60-min factors). Daily gz backup to `<db dir>/backups/watch-*.db.gz` (keep 1, old copies dropped first, skipped when free space is short) + bucket `watch/`. **Daily auto-report** (`report_if_due`, own thread, once per UTC date in `watch_state.last_report`): `write_report(date)` -> `<db dir>/reports/watch-DATE/` (`status.json`, `report.txt`/`report.json` for pump2x/clean2x/pump5x, `rows-{5,15,30,60}m.csv.gz`; keep 3 locally) uploaded to bucket `watch/reports/watch-DATE/`. `download_links(client, bucket)` = 7-day presigned URLs for the newest report + newest db backup. Live-verified 2026-10-14: ~20 new pools per 40s; first day on Fly ~157 pools/hour-ish, info fetches lag snapshots slightly |
| `tradingbot/watch_analysis.py` | What predicts a pump. `build_dataset(store, age)`: one row per pool with a snapshot at `age` and a finished 24h window; features only from data knowable at the snapshot (snapshot numbers, ratios like buyer_ratio/trades_per_trader/vol_to_liq, price vs first seen and previous snapshot, static name/dex/hour, token info only if `info_ts` ≤ snapshot ts, developer prior launches + prior pump rate from finished earlier tokens). Labels from all pools of the same mint (candles + snapshot prices), judged on 5-min CLOSES (wicks ignored): `pump2x`, `pump5x`, `clean2x` (close ≥2x before low ≤0.5x), `trade_ret` (TP +100% at close, SL -50% filled at the stop or the gapped close, 24h exit, 6% round-trip cost); `best_mult` (high) and `best_close_mult` kept. `Context` features merged per row. `analyse(..., subset="enriched")` = rows with Helius data (`--enriched` on the CLI); `subset="control"` (`--control`) = rows whose Helius lookup was `control`/`all`; rows carry `hel_selection`/`x_selection` (ID keys, from `Context.selection`). `paid_value(rows)` (`--paid-value`; top of the daily report, 15-min rows): on the unbiased slice, held-out AUC/lift with vs without the features only that source provides, plus pump rate of ranked vs control picks. The daily report covers all + enriched. `analyse()`: univariate AUC + quintile pump rates, L2 logistic regression (numpy) trained on earliest 70% by time, held-out AUC, top-decile lift and trade returns. `summary()` per age 5/15/30/60. CLI `watch-status`, `watch-report [--age N] [--label pump2x|pump5x|clean2x] [--json]`, `watch-export PATH [--age N]`, `watch-report-now`, `watch-links` |
| `tradingbot/enrich.py` | Watcher enrichment on its own `enricher` thread (`enrich_poll_s`=20; started with the watcher when `cfg.enrich`). Periodic: `poll_lists` (DexScreener boosts top/latest + profiles latest, GeckoTerminal trending 5m/1h with ranks -> `watch_lists`), `poll_market` (SOL price + new pools per interval incl. unsampled, from `Watcher.seen_counts` -> `watch_market`), `poll_telegram` (`tg_channels` t.me previews -> `watch_mentions`). Per pool (`WINDOWS` in minutes; stored in `watch_enrich(pool, kind, ts, data)`, '{}' = tried): `pumpfun5`/`pumpfun15` (coin state + creator history at 5), `orders` (DexScreener paid products), `narrative` (Haiku tags, `NARRATIVE_SCHEMA`), `image` (dHash + sha1), `helius` (7-60 min; first 100 txs oldest-first -> per-owner token deltas, top-20 holders + owners + supply, early buyers' wallet origin cached in `watch_wallets`), `x` (counts + 10 posts). Also: `jupiter` (all pools), and for eligible pools `rugcheck`, `jupquote` (round trip $100, impact $500), `links` (Telegram/Discord members, website, domain age), `crosschain` (same ticker on other chains / older Solana tokens), `dev` (pump.fun profile, best of 3 prior coins' ATH, Helius creator origin), `sentiment` (Haiku on X posts), `candles15` (1-min candles); periodic `poll_macro` (-> `watch_macro`, `jup:trending_1h` list, `perp_symbols` state), `poll_biz` (4chan -> `watch_mentions` channel `biz`), `poll_xfeed` (`x_watch_accounts` timelines -> `watch_xfeed`). Narrative schema has 22 fields incl. image reading (vision). `PAID_OR_HEAVY` tasks only for `eligible` pools (`is_graduation`: pumpswap, a `pump`/`bonk` mint, or a launchpad pool of the same mint in `watch_pools`; every other pool needs `buyers_m5` ≥ `enrich_min_buyers_m5`=20). **Paid-lookup targeting (2026-10-15):** `MINT_KINDS` (helius, x, dev, narrative, sentiment) are copied from another pool of the same mint when one has a usable row (`copied_from`, source ts kept, helius `pool_accounts` gains the new pool; free). `RANKED` kinds (helius, x) go through `select()`: `free_score` from snap5 (log buyers + buyer ratio + vol/liq), coverage f = budget per hour / avg cost (`avgcost:<kind>` state, EMA) ÷ eligible per hour over `enrich_rank_window_h`=6h; stable hash < `enrich_control_share`=0.2·f -> `selection: control`; else score ≥ the (1−0.8f) quantile -> `ranked`; else saved as `{skipped: 'below rank threshold', score, threshold, coverage}`; under `enrich_rank_min_history`=30 scored rows or f ≥ 1 -> `all`. `status()['paid_selection_24h']` counts each (status counts cover the last 24h via index `watch_enrich_ts`; all-time counts read every Helius blob and timed out). `PER_KIND_PER_STEP`=6, `TASK_LIMIT_PER_STEP`=40. Budgets `Budget` (daily, pro-rata per UTC hour, `watch_state` keys `spend:<name>:<date>`): `helius_daily_credits`=30000, `x_daily_budget_usd`=3, `narrative_daily_usd`=2; out of budget = task left for later (no row). Keys: `HELIUS_API_KEY`, `X_BEARER_TOKEN`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`; missing key = `{"skipped": ...}`. **Rug catcher data (`operator`, 10-29):** first task each pass, window 5-10 min, every pool whose 5-min snapshot has liq ≥ `operator_min_liq_usd`=5000 (else `{skipped: 'below liquidity'}`): Helius pool's first tx -> `pool_creation` (creator = fee payer, create_ts, seed_sol = pool's wSOL after creation, create_ix), creator's `wallet_first_ts`/`funder` (cached in `watch_wallets`), and its earlier pools in this DB (operator rows with the same creator; same-funder count as `funder_prior_pools`): for up to `OPERATOR_PULL_CHECKS`=5 of them not yet checked (or un-pulled and checked >30 min ago) `Helius.recent(pool, 50)` -> `find_withdraw` (log `Instruction: Withdraw`/`RemoveLiquidity`…, not `WithdrawFees`) -> table `watch_pulls(pool, checked_ts, pull_ts, by, sol_out)`. Row: prior_pools, prior_pulled, pull_age_min/median (minutes after launch), history [[created_ts, pull_ts, checked_ts]…]. Own budget `operator_daily_credits`=20000 (`helius_daily_credits` lowered to 10000; 30k/day total), worst case 70 credits/lookup. `status()`: `operator_24h` (known/new operator, skipped), `pulls_found`. **Launch data (`launch`, 11-01):** `poll_launch`, see the 11-01 status entry. Parsers verified on Google AI's real create/pull txs. Stage 2 (after ~a week): walk-forward rug-in-hold model on these + snapshot features; stage 3: live blind predictions logged per signal before outcomes, compared in the report, no trading effect. **Creators of all pumpswap pools (10-29):** `poll_creators` every pass reads up to `CREATORS_PER_STEP`=500 pumpswap pools without a row, newest first, via `Helius.pool_creators` (getMultipleAccounts, dataSlice offset 11 len 32 = creator in the pumpswap Pool account, owner `PUMPSWAP_PROGRAM`; 1 credit/100 pools, public RPC when no key; `b58encode`) -> table `watch_creators(pool, creator, ts)` (creator NULL = not a pumpswap pool); `status()['creators_known']` |
| `tradingbot/data/pumpfun.py`, `helius.py`, `xapi.py`, `telegram.py` | Enrichment clients, all fail soft. pump.fun: `frontend-api-v3.pump.fun/coins-v2/{mint}` (is_currently_live, ath_market_cap in USD, security_verdict, boost_mode, transfer fee/hook, creator, bonding_curve) and `/coins-v2/user-created-coins/{creator}` (count + coins with created_timestamp/complete; market caps there are 0). Helius: `getTransactionsForAddress` (sortOrder asc, full, 10 credits/100 tx), `getTokenLargestAccounts`, `getTokenSupply`, `getMultipleAccounts`; `trades()` -> [blockTime, slot, owner, delta, fee_payer]; `funder_of()`. **Backup key (11-08):** Fly secret `BACKUP_HELIUS_API_KEY` (`helius.BACKUP_ENV`); when the main key answers Helius's out-of-credits reply (HTTP 429 'max usage reached', or 402/403 with a usage message: `out_of_credits`), every client on the default key switches at once (`KEYS`, `_switch`, `current_key()`, `Helius.key()`), retrying the same call on the backup; one call per `PROBE_S`=3600 s tries the main again and switches back when it answers; a main key that answers 429 to every retry (1+2+4+8 s back-off, no credits text needed) also switches, with the next probe after `RATE_PROBE_S`=600 s; a single 429 that clears on retry doesn't switch; every non-200 now logs the first 200 characters of the reply and which key (main/backup); clients given an explicit key (the history backfill) never switch; no main key = backup used. Also used by the launch websocket URL (reconnects when the key changes), `Jupiter._decimals_onchain` and the live executor's `rpc_url`; `launch-status` websocket section shows `helius_key` (`key_status()`). Tests `tests/test_helius_backup.py`. X: `/2/tweets/counts/recent`, `/2/tweets/search/recent` with author public_metrics; query = quoted mint, no retweets. Telegram: `parse_page()` regex over `data-post` blocks. DexScreener gained `lists()` and `orders(token)`; GeckoTerminal `trending_ranked(duration)`, `sol_price()` |
| `tradingbot/data/pumpswap.py` | Pumpswap pool / LP-mint account parsing from raw bytes (see the 11-02 phase 1 entry) |
| `tradingbot/launch_live.py` | **Second-4 launch scorer (`launch_live`, on in fly.toml); its top-2% / top-5% picks feed the `launch4_2` / `launch4_5` paper books (`launch_book`).** At the decision, picks go onto the engine's model-signal queue (`signals`, `kind: launch`, price = chain price at second 4 x SOL price, liq = 2 x SOL x SOL price, `top2`/`top5`, `decided_ts`). `LaunchLive.start()` (engine, with the watcher): websocket thread `launch-ws` (`programSubscribe` Token-2022 `dataSize` 82 = pumpswap LP mints, confirmed; Helius wss with the key, else the public node; reconnect back-off to 60 s) -> `on_lp_mint` (new mint with supply > 0, LRU `seen`; supply 0 on a held pool sets `pulled_ts`) -> scheduler thread `launch-live` + 4 workers: `_new` (pool account via `Helius.accounts`, 1 credit: pumpswap owner, SOL pair, LP mint matches, `handmade`, token mint not `…pump`; skips only counted) -> `_decide` at notification + `launch_live_decide_delay_s`=4 (Helius `history_until`, ≤2 pages, empty history retried `EMPTY_RETRIES`=2 x 1.5 s; the backtest's own `launch_record` + `live_features` = `_static_features` without a name + `launch_features` at `DECIDE_S`=4; skips: too late (>20 s after creation), nothing by second 4, pages ended before second 4, not liquid (2 x SOL x `watch_market` SOL price < `model_book_min_liq_usd`, or pulled)) -> score with `model/launch4.json`, `top2`/`top5` = score ≥ the training scores' 98th/95th percentile -> `_fill` at create + `FILL_S`=6 + 0.5 s (vault read, 1 credit: SOL / raw token units) -> `_exit` at create + `EXIT_S`=360 (ret = exit/fill - 1 - 6%, capped +300%; -100% if pool SOL < 10% of its peak, LP supply hit 0, or no price). Rug-vs-win filter (11-04): `RugWinModel` / `model/rw.json`, `_rw_verdict` + `rw_flags` feed `launchrw50` / `launchd12rw50` (see the 11-04 filter-study entry). **Since 11-05 they follow the study's rule:** trained on every finished pool (`train_rw`, no 14-day window), retrained every `RW_BLOCK_S`=6 h by `_rw_retrain_loop` (thread `rw-retrain`, niced child `launch-rw-train`, checks every `RW_RETRY_S`=600 s), each pool ranked against the past 6 h (`rw_all`/`rw_low` deques), launchd12rw50's bottom 20% = `rw_low6` (history score in the past 6 h's bottom 20%; `rw_low(r)` falls back to `low_h` on older rows); the launchd12 books keep `low_h` (24 h). **Twin books on the earlier rule (11-05):** `model_book.LAUNCH_RW24_CUT` = `launchrw50_24h` / `launchd12rw50_24h` (flags `rw50_24` / `d12rw50_24`, `rw24_flags`), config `launch_rw24_book` (fly.toml `TB_LAUNCH_RW24_BOOK=1`): own model `model/rw24.json` (`train_rw24_and_save` = `train_rw(..., days=launch_train_days)`, CLI `launch-rw24-train`, retrained every `RW24_RETRAIN_S`=24 h by the same `_rw_retrain_loop`), ranked against the past `RW24_WINDOW_S`=24 h, bottom 20% = `low_h`; `_load_rw_model(track)` / `_rw_verdict(..., track='rw24')` keep separate state (`rw24_model`, `rw24_all`, `rw24_low`) and columns `rw24`, `rw24_pct`, `rw24_low_pct`; `launch-status` groups `rug-vs-win 24 h rule: ...`. **Thin pools (11-08):** config `launch_live_track_thin` (default on): a pool under `model_book_min_liq_usd` at second 4 that wasn't pulled gets status `thin` and the same fill/exit vault reads (~2 credits) -> `thin_done` with `ret` (`OUTCOME_STATUSES`); no flags, no signal, never in training, rw rankings or the creator record (those read `scored`/`done`); only `pool_bands` / the market monitor's `bands` use them. **By-band rule (11-08):** `_rw_verdict(..., liq=)` on the `rw` track also ranks the score against the past `BAND_WINDOW_S`=24 h's liquid pools of the same band (`rw_band` deques per `BAND_RULES` band, rebuilt from stored rows on every rw model load; `rwb_pct`, None until `ROLLING_MIN` pools) -> column `rwb_pct`; `band_flags(liq_usd, rw_pct, rwb_pct)` -> `rwband` per `BAND_RULES` ($5-15k band rank < 80, $15-25k band rank < 30, $25-40k live `rw_pct` < 50, else never; `band_of`); signals go out for `rwband` too; dashboard and `fill_gap` know the flag. **+50% filter in $40-80k (11-08, `launch_big_book`):** `BIG_BAND` $40-80k, `train_big`/`train_big_and_save` -> `model/big50.json` (logistic "+50% or more by minute 6" via `_fit_capped` on every finished liquid pool, meta `band_quantiles` from the band's training pools of the last day; CLI `launch-big-train`, retrained every `RW_BLOCK_S` by `_rw_retrain_loop`, also in `watch-model-train`); `_big_verdict` scores $40-80k pools only and ranks them against the band's past `BIG_WINDOW_S`=24 h (`big_band` deque, rebuilt on model load) -> columns `big`, `big_pct` (higher = better odds); `big_flags(liq, big_pct)` -> `big30` (best `BIG_KEEP`=30%), sent as a signal, known to the dashboard and `fill_gap`. Table `watch_launch_live` (one row per hand-made pool, prediction stored before the outcome; statuses pending/scored/done/thin/thin_done/skipped/lost; `_resume` re-arms due reads after a restart). Budget `launch_live_daily_credits`=20000 (~13 credits per pool). Counts per UTC day + websocket stats in `watch_state` (`launch_live:counts:<date>`, `launch_live:ws`). `train`/`train_and_save` (in `watch-model-train` when `launch_live`, nightly): `la.rows` over `launch_train_days`=14, sampled pools liquid at second 4 with a second-6 return, names minus `LIVE_DROP`, label second-6 return > 0, needs `MIN_TRAIN`=200. CLI `launch-status [--hours 24]`: counts, websocket, credits, statuses, skip reasons, timing medians (notified / decided / history indexed to / fill, seconds after creation), picks, outcomes (every liquid pool / top 2% / top 5% with 90% CI, rugged %), feature parity vs the enricher's later launch row for the same pool (sampled pools), model meta |
| `tradingbot/market_monitor.py` | **Always-on meme market monitor (11-07, read only, never trades)**, config `market_monitor` (fly.toml `TB_MARKET_MONITOR=1`, needs `launch_live`): thread `market-monitor` every `MONITOR_POLL_S`=600 s on its own WatchStore connection; `MarketMonitor.step` writes one row per finished UTC hour (`HOUR_DELAY_S`=15 min after it ends; the first run backfills from the scorer's first pool) to watch.db `market_hours(hour, data, ts)` via `hour_metrics(pools, trades, prices, now)`: supply (pools, liquid, liquid %, median liquidity and seed SOL at second 4), liquidity bands (`BANDS` = sz's: under $15k / $15-25k / $25k+: share of liquid pools, ideal mean per band, thin-band rug % and thin-band rw50 picks' ideal mean), operators (distinct creators, top-5 creators' share, repeat creators %, `template_pct` = pools on a seed size (rounded to 1 SOL) shared by ≥3 pools that hour), rugs (rug % by minute 6, median LP pull second, pulled < 90 s %, rugged later %), buying (median buy SOL and buyers in the first 4 s, +50% winners %, market ideal mean/median), edge (market sized like sz, rug-vs-win kept/skipped half and gap on live `rw_pct`, sz picks at ideal sized, sz real trades and size-weighted result from the journal (open > 30 min = -100%), fill gap), wider (SOL/BTC close and hourly change from `watch_market`/`watch_macro`, fear & greed). `compare(rows, since=)`: last `RECENT_H`=3 hours vs the trailing `BASELINE_H`=7 days (no earlier than config `market_monitor_since`, fly.toml `TB_MARKET_MONITOR_SINCE`=11-05 03:00 UTC when the Helius budget was uncapped; `sz_*` measures only from the sized book's first trade), z = (recent - mean of 3-hour windows) / their spread, pooled metrics weighted by count (`WEIGHT`), needs `MIN_BASELINE_WINDOWS`=24. Rows carry `v`; rows from an older `VERSION` (now 3: adds `sz_pnl`/`sz_cost`) are rebuilt on the next step. **Saved flags (11-07, step 3):** after storing hours `step` runs `update_flags()` (result in `MarketMonitor.events`, for the Telegram step): per new hour `advance_flags(state, hour, cmp)` on `compare` of the rows up to that hour; a flag opens when a measure's |z| ≥ `FLAG_Z`=2 for `FLAG_HOURS`=2 hours in a row in the same direction (dated from the first hot hour), updates its peak, closes at |z| < `CLOSE_Z`=1.5 or a sign flip; a missing z keeps an open flag open; `FLAG_SKIP` (sz_trades, sol_usd, btc_usd, fear_greed) never flag. Table `market_flags(id, metric, direction, opened_hour, closed_hour, peak_z, data)`; progress in `watch_state` `market_flags:last`/`:state`, and `market_flags:key` (VERSION:since) rebuilds every flag when either changes. `read_flags(query)`, `flag_summary(rows, flags, now, days=FLAG_DAYS 7)`: open flags and those closed in 7 days with sz's size-weighted result, trades and the market mean in the flagged hours, plus per measure sz in vs out of flagged hours (`sz_gap`). CLI render: OPEN FLAGS, FLAGS CLOSED IN THE LAST 7 DAYS, WHAT THE FLAGS MEANT FOR SZ; dashboard Market tab 'Saved flags' card (payload `flags`, `flag_rule`). **Status (11-08):** `step` wraps `_step` and writes watch_state `STATUS_KEY`=`market_monitor:status` ({ran, ok_ts, took, stored, error, error_ts}; the last success is kept on failure); `read_status(query)` feeds `payload(..., status=)`; the Market tab's stale note names the cause: dashboard read failing (`built_ts` > 10 min old), no recorded run (thread never started), no run for 3 polls (thread stopped/stuck), last run failed (shows the error), or ran clean with nothing new. `build_many(hours)` reads each table once per `BATCH_HOURS`=24 missing hours and buckets by hour (11-07: one query set per hour scanned whole un-indexed tables and the first ~120-hour backfill ran 10+ min). **Impaired hours (11-09):** `impairment()` marks hours with no pools or ≥ 20% of pools skipped for non-market reasons; `healthy()` leaves them out of compare, flags, band totals and charts (see the 11-09 entry). CLI `market-monitor [--hours 24] [--json]` (fills missing hours first, then the hourly table + the comparison, |z| ≥ 2 marked). `read(query, since)` (read-only, no table = []) + `payload(rows)` (last `PAGE_HOURS`=72 rows, `compare`, metric list, `last_hour`) feed the dashboard's Market tab (`Dashboard.fetch_market`, thread `dashboard-market` every `MARKET_POLL_S`=60 s on its own read-only watch.db connection, stream event `market`, init `market_enabled`). Tests `tests/test_market_monitor.py`, `tests/test_dashboard.py` |

| `tradingbot/risk_history.py` | **Books replayed over the pool history (11-08, read only)**, CLI `risk-model --history [--retrain-h 24] [--json]`: `load_pools` = `hist.db` rows (`launch_hist.rows`, streamed via `WatchStore.iterate`) + the scorer's finished liquid rows (preferred per pool, carry the live `rw_pct`); a creator's earlier pools recounted over the whole set (`repeat_creator`, `log_prior_pools`), creator record via `launch_live._creator_history` (LP pull times from the backfill), inputs `pull_inputs`; feature dicts dropped after. `replay`: walk-forward rug-vs-win (`_Fit`: median-filled, standardised, clipped logistic; first fit `FIT_ITERS`=1500, later fits warm-started from the previous day's weights for `WARM_ITERS`=250) retrained every `retrain_h` (default 24 h) on at most `MAX_TRAIN`=60,000 earlier finished pools (fixed random sample), each pool scored by the model of its time; `rw_pct` = rank among the past 6 h (fewer than ROLLING_MIN: rank among that fit's training scores, counted as thin), `rwb_pct` = rank among the past 24 h of its BAND_RULES band. `RULES`: every liquid pool / launchrw50 / launchrw50_sz / launchrwband / launchrwband_sz; trades = ideal ret minus each book's execution gap (`gaps`: journal real-minus-ideal via `band_compare._trades` when ≥ `GAP_MIN`=30 matched, else `DEFAULT_GAP`=2.4 pts), sized like the book capped at the pool share; then `risk_model` stats, trade-size bootstrap, correlation, plus `monthly` per rule and `check` (replay vs live rw_pct on shared pools). **Sampled backfill (11-08):** `sample_share` = pools fetched (any status) / pools in `hist_candidates` x the parsed share; history rows before the scorer's first pool get `w` = 1/share (scorer days w = 1); ranking windows widen by w; `expand(ts)` rebuilds each sampled day to n x w trades, drawing from the day's own trades with chance k, else from +-`LOCAL_DAYS`=7 days (k = 1 - mean(var/n) / variance of day means around their local mean: the real share of day-to-day swings), so daily P&L keeps real swings without multiplying sampling noise; `risk_model.book_stats(..., per_trade=)` takes per-trade measures from the sampled trades weighted by w (bootstrap chunked to bound memory); output `sample`. **Minute-15 band rule (11-09, `--hold15`):** adds books `HOLD_RULES` = sz with its $25-40k kept pools (`hold_candidates`: replay `rw_pct` < 50 in `launch_live.HOLD_BAND`) held to pool minute 15 at min(2% of pool, `HOLD_MAX_USD` $500), the rest sz as is (`hold_trades`), twice: as measured and with extra impact `HOLD_IMPACT_RT` 4 x (size - sz's size) / liquidity; gap = launchrw50_sz's. The history holds no minute-15 price, so `fetch_holds` reads each candidate's state after its last tx by minute 15 (`Helius.last_before`, 10 credits, live key, `--rps`, `--max-credits` default 80k; skips pools rugged by 6 or LP-pulled by 15; stable hash order) into hist.db `hist_path(pool, sec, sol, tok_ui, tok_raw)` (display units for history rows, whose `entry_px` comes from `launch_hist.rows`; raw for scorer rows, whose `fill_px` is per raw unit); `hold_ret` = price / entry - 1 - 6%, capped +300%, -100% when rugged by 6, LP pulled by 15, no balance / emptied, or SOL < 10% of the minute-6 peak; a missing state keeps the minute-6 result (counted). Output `hold15`. Tests `tests/test_risk_history.py` |

Books: launchrw50_sz, launchrwband_sz (`risk_history.rule_trades`) and, when `hist_path` holds minute-15 states, sz with the $25-40k hold (`risk_history.hold_trades`). Causal rules (`rules()`; at a trade's decision only pools finished before it, `market_window` / `_Window` prefix sums by finish time, and the book's trades already sold): market Hh < T (every liquid pool's ideal mean over the past H h; `MARKET` 6 h -5..-20%, 24 h -5..-15%), rugs Hh > T (`RUGS`), edge 24h < T (kept minus skipped half), own Hh < T (the book's own size-weighted result, `OWN`), day stop k% (rest of the UTC day once the realized day loss passes k% of the median daily money traded, `DAYSTOP`), and half-size versions; `MIN_POOLS`=20 / `MIN_TRADES`=20 or no signal. `score`: $ (weighted by w), mean, taken %, max DD, worst day/month, bad months (the book's own months < 0) $ and saved %, good months $ and kept %, per month. | `tradingbot/sz_stops.py` | **Stop losses and climb checks on a book's real trades (11-09, read only; Helius only with `--fetch`)**, CLI `sz-stops [--book launchrw50_sz] [--hours N] [--fetch] [--max-credits 15000] [--rps 2] [--json]`: `rules()` = `STOPS` -10..-50% below the pool price at our buy, `TRAILS` -20/-30/-40% below the best since, `CLIMBS` (1/2/3/4 min after the buy x +0/5/10%: sell unless a price seen since the buy beat the bar), each checked every `CHECK_S`=5 s on the pool price, selling `SELL_DELAY_S`=3 s later. Paths: `fetch_paths` reads each traded pool's txs from buy-`PRE_S` to sale+`POST_S` (getTransactionsForAddress blockTime gte/lte, 10 credits/100 txs, `MAX_PAGES`=8, stable hash order so a cap leaves an even sample) into watch.db `watch_trade_path(pool, data, ts)` (rows [blockTime, pool SOL, raw tokens], `complete`); paths cut by the page cap are not judged (`coverage.cut_by_page_cap`). Exited return = real + (proceeds per token at the rule's sale - at the real sale) / buy price, proceeds with our own constant-product sale impact from the reserves at that moment (`_keep`; ours = cost / SOL price (scorer liq_usd / 2 / sol_at_decide) / buy price; bigger impact after an LP withdrawal, smaller after a sell-off). Per rule: $, change vs no rule with a 90% paired bootstrap CI over trades, each half, 6 h blocks helped/hurt, saved / cut-winners $, fires on rugs, by liquidity band, mean, max DD; `path_check` (pool proceeds minus real return, ~+6..+10 pts expected); list of rules whose CI clears 0 and help in both halves. **Drawdown limits (11-09, user):** `limits()` = day loss `DAY_LOSS` 1/2/3/5/7.5/10% and day trail `DAY_TRAIL` 1/2/3/5/7.5/10% of `CAPITAL_USD` $10k (`dd_limits.apply_rule`: no buys for the rest of the UTC day once hit), alone (`limits` with CIs; they need no path, so they act on every trade) and on top of every exit rule (`combos`: the matrix of $ change, and the best `TOP` by $/max DD among combinations where the limit changed something, with CIs); `rule_return` returns the sale time, so an early sale counts toward the day when its money came back. Tests `tests/test_sz_stops.py` |

Per band, every pool and the rug-vs-win kept half (`rw_pct` < 50): mean / CI / median / win / rugged / +50% per hold and the paired gap vs minute 6 with a 90% CI, plus `rug causes` per hold (`path_detail`: LP pulled / SOL drained / pool emptied / no balance found (the pool's last tx didn't list its vaults: possibly a data gap) / rugged by minute 6). `--kept-only` samples only the kept half (`_kept`), `--max-liq` drops bigger pools. SOL pairs only) -> per pool `Helius.first_time(lp_mint)` (oldest of the LP mint's signatures = creation, 1 credit) -> outside the window: saved `outside window`; inside: `Helius.history_from_start(pool, SPAN_S=400, MAX_PAGES=6)` -> `launch_record` -> table `watch_launch_hist(pool, token_mint, lp_mint, create_ts, status, data, ts)`; | `tradingbot/model_book.py` | **model5 paper book (no Claude).** `train()` = `training_rows()` (pools finished in the last `model_train_days`=4, their 5-min snapshot, 24h labels from `watch_analysis._future_paths`/`_outcome`) on `FEATURES` = `_static_features` + `_snap_features` + `log_price_vs_first` (41 snapshot-only factors: walk-forward as good as the full 150), L2 logistic, cut-off = top `model_book_top_pct`=2% of the last 2 days' scores; `train_and_save` -> `<db dir>/model/model5.json` (CLI `watch-model-train`, run daily in the report child before the report; 10 s / ~0.5 GB on 12 days). `ModelBook.consider(pool, age, snap)` is the watcher's `on_snapshot` listener: age 5 only, score ≥ cut-off, liquidity ≥ `model_book_min_liq_usd`=5000 -> `signals` queue (reloads the model file every 5 min). Engine `model` thread (`MODEL_POLL_S`=5) -> `buy_model_signals`: skips signals older than `MODEL_SIGNAL_MAX_AGE_S`=180, held tokens, > `model_book_max_open`=120, daily loss ≥ `model_book_daily_loss_pct`=15; size min(`model_book_usd`=100, 2% of liquidity, cash); Jupiter live price (drift ≤ `model_book_max_drift_pct`=25), round trip ≤ `max_round_trip_loss_pct`; fills via `SimExecutor`; events `model_buy` / `entry_skipped`. **Two books trade the same signals with separate cash and ledgers (10-28):** `NAME`=`model5` (max hold `model_book_hold_min`=1440, 24h) and `FAST`=`model5_10m` (`model_book_fast_hold_min`=10; on when `model_book_fast`; its executor is a `copy.copy` of model5's); `BOOKS`, `hold_min(cfg, name)`, `exit_config(cfg, name)` (+100% TP all, -50% stop, the book's hold) via `Book.exit_cfg`; each starts at `model_book_cash_usd`=10000. `buy_model_signals` offers every signal to each book (own skip checks, quotes and fills); only `model5_10m` skips pools older than `model_book_max_entry_age_min`=10 ('pool too old for the short hold'; signals carry `created_ts` and `dex`). **Pool-age exit (10-28):** `is_handmade(dex, mint)` = pumpswap pool of a mint not ending in `pump` (creator holds the LP and can pull it); `exit_deadline(cfg, name, sig)` = created + `model_book_handmade_exit_age_min`=15 min for `model5_10m` only (0 = off); engine stores it as event `model_exit_deadline` (`{position_id, deadline}`, reloaded by `Engine._load_deadlines` after restarts) and `manage_positions` sells with reason `pool_age_exit` once it passes, if no other exit fired. **Third book `CLIMB`=`model5_climb` (10-28):** FAST's rules (10-min hold, entry-age guard, pool-age exit; `SHORT`=(FAST, CLIMB)) plus `climb_check(cfg, name, bought_ts, market_price)`: `model_book_climb_after_min`=3 min after the buy, sell with reason `no_climb` unless the price has reached `model_book_climb_min_gain_pct`=5% above the buy's market (trigger) price; a pass ends the check. Engine keeps `_climbs[pos_id] = [at, target, best seen]` (event `model_climb_check`, reloaded by `_load_climbs`; best seen restarts at 0), `_climb_failed` in `manage_positions`. `active_books(cfg)` = model5 if `model_book_slow` + FAST if `model_book_fast` + CLIMB if `model_book_climb`; `manage_positions` only handles positions of running books (a switched-off book's positions stay in the journal untouched: no prices, no sell quotes). **Rug catcher blind test (10-29):** `operator_verdict(store, pool)` = `new wallet` (creator has no earlier pool in `watch_creators`), `repeat operator`, or `unknown` (not pumpswap / creator not read yet); `ModelBook.consider` puts it on each signal as `rug_check` (`ModelBook.store` = the watcher's store, set by the engine); `buy_model_signals` logs event `rug_prediction` (strategy `model`, once per signal) before any book trades; trading is unchanged. `rug_catcher_summary(journal, books)` groups each short book's trades on those tokens (bought within 10 min of the prediction) by verdict: signals, trades, P&L, per trade, rugged (≤ -80%, or still open 40+ min); report section `RUG CATCHER`. `rug_summary(journal, stuck, name)`. **Operator books `OPS9`=`model5_ops9`, `OPS10`=`model5_ops10` (10-29):** trade only signals whose `rug_check` verdict is `repeat operator` (`verdict_skip`: others skipped as 'rug catcher: <verdict>'), hold `OPS_HOLD_MIN` 9 / 10 min, entry-age guard, NO pool-age exit (`AGE_EXIT`=(FAST, CLIMB)), no climb check; `SHORT`=(FAST, CLIMB, OPS9, OPS10); switch `model_book_ops` (both). `operator_verdict` returns `graduation` for pumpswap pools of `…pump` mints (each migration pool has a fresh creator, so they used to read as `new wallet`). `operator_verdict(store, pool, helius)`: non-pumpswap pools -> `unknown` with reason `not a pumpswap pool (<dex>)`; a pumpswap pool whose creator isn't in `watch_creators` yet is read on the spot (`ModelBook.helius`, a 5 s-timeout `Helius` client set by the engine; runs on the watcher thread) and stored. RUG CATCHER groups unknowns as `unknown: not read` / `unknown: other dex`. **Early books `EARLY5`/`EARLY6`/`EARLY7` = `early5`/`early6`/`early7` (10-31, `early_book`, off by default, on in fly.toml):** the watcher's `on_discover(pool, first)` hook (called for every newly recorded pool, first-sight GeckoTerminal numbers) -> `ModelBook.consider_first`: hand-made pumpswap pools only (`is_handmade`), first seen ≤ `EARLY_MAX_SEEN_MIN`=4 min after creation, scored by the early model (`early_features` = `_static_features` + `_snap_features(first)` + `seen_age_min`; `train_early` on `early_training_rows`: label `up` = 5-min snapshot price ≥ first × 1.10 after the 6% round trip and liquidity not < 10% of first; cut-off = top `early_top_pct`=5% of the last 2 days; `model/early.json`, trained by `watch-model-train` when `early_book`), liq ≥ `model_book_min_liq_usd` -> signal with `kind: early` (carries `seen_ts`; every book buys it in parallel, events `early_timing`). `buy_model_signals` sends early signals only to the early books and model5 signals only to the rest; `exit_deadline` = created + 5/6/7 min (`EARLY_EXIT_MIN`), `hold_min` 5/6/7 as a backstop; skip 'pool too old to sell by minute N' when the pool is within 30 s of its deadline. `early_status(store, cfg, hours)` / CLI `early-status [--hours 8]`: funnel over the window (pools seen, hand-made, seen-age p10/median/p90, seen within 4 min, enough liquidity), first-sight scores (p50/p90/p95/max, count ≥ cut-off, the cut-off that would give the top 5% of these) and per-book buys/skips. `early_backtest(store, cfg, hours)` / CLI `early-backtest [--hours 8]`: the window's eligible pools (`early_candidates`: hand-made, seen within 4 min, enough liquidity, scored by the current early model) under the fixed cut-off vs a rolling one (`rolling_cut_off`: the (100 - `early_top_pct`) percentile of eligible scores in the previous `ROLLING_WINDOW_S`=24 h, needs `ROLLING_MIN_SCORES`=50); outcome = 5-minute snapshot (~pool minute 6.3) vs first-sight price, 6% round trip, emptied (liq < 10% of first) = -100%; coarser than the 1-min candle backtest. **Launch books `LAUNCH2`/`LAUNCH5` = `launch4_2`/`launch4_5` (11-02, `launch_book` + `launch_live`, on in fly.toml; twins `launch4h_2`/`launch4h_5` on the history-trained model with a rolling 24 h cut-off, `launch_hist_book`, see the 11-03 entry):** take `kind: launch` signals from `launch_live` (`signal_kind(name)` routes early / launch / model5 signals, `wants(name, sig)` = the signal's `top2`/`top5` flag); bought through the normal model path (Jupiter live price or quote price, round trip, `SimExecutor` quote -> latency -> re-quote -> revert; no 25% drift skip for launch signals, as the backtest bought at second 6 whatever the move); `exit_deadline` = created + 6 min (`LAUNCH_EXIT_MIN`, `POOL_MINUTE_EXIT` = early + launch pool-minute exits, also used for the 'pool too old to sell by minute N' skip); `exit_config` = no stop / no take-profit (stop 99%, TP 10,000%); `early_timing(journal, since, books, LAUNCH_STAGES)` gives created->decided->picked->started->filled. **By-band twins `LAUNCH_BAND`/`LAUNCH_BAND_SZ` = `launchrwband`/`launchrwband_sz` (11-08, config `launch_band_book`, fly.toml `TB_LAUNCH_BAND_BOOK=1`):** `LAUNCH_BAND_CUT` flag `rwband` (part of `LAUNCH_CUT`: minute-6 exit, no stop, 10% sell slippage), sized by `band_usd` over `BAND_SIZE_BANDS`: $100 everywhere, except $250 in $5-15k pools for the `_sz` twin (still capped at 2% of the pool and the 5% impact cap); dashboard family Rug-vs-win, report `EXPECTED`. The older rw books keep running unchanged as the comparison. **Hold twins `LAUNCH_HOLD10`/`LAUNCH_HOLD15` = `launchrw50_25k10`/`launchrw50_25k15` (+ `LAUNCH_HOLD15_MAX`=`launchrw50_25k15max`, minute 15 at $500, 11-09) (11-08, config `launch_hold_book`, fly.toml `TB_LAUNCH_HOLD_BOOK=1`):** `LAUNCH_HOLD_CUT` flag `rw50big` (`launch_live.band_flags`: live `rw_pct` < 50 and liquidity at second 4 in `launch_live.HOLD_BAND` $25-40k; signals go out for it), part of `LAUNCH_CUT` (no stop, 10% sell slippage) but `LAUNCH_EXIT_MIN` 10 / 15 (pool-minute deadline), $250 a trade via `BAND_SIZE_BANDS` (2% of pool cap still applies); dashboard family Rug-vs-win, report `EXPECTED`. The dashboard's and `fill_gap`'s ideal for these books is the scorer's minute-6 result, so their real-vs-ideal gap includes the hold difference. **Conditional-hold book `LAUNCH_BIG`=`launchbig40k` (11-08, config `launch_big_book`, fly.toml `TB_LAUNCH_BIG_BOOK=1`):** flag `big30` (`LAUNCH_BIG_CUT`, part of `LAUNCH_CUT`), $250 via `BAND_SIZE_BANDS`, `LAUNCH_EXIT_MIN` 15; `HOLD_CHECK_MIN` = pool minutes 6 and 10: `hold_checks(name, sig)` gives the times, the engine (`Engine._holds`, event `model_hold_check`, reloaded by `_load_holds`; a missed check runs once on restart) quotes a sale of the position at each (`_confirm_sell_price`) and `hold_check_fails(ret, last)` sells (reason `hold_check`, event `hold_check_result` with the return) when proceeds/cost - 1 < `HOLD_CHECK_GAIN`=0 or below the previous check (`HOLD_CHECK_RISING`); a failed quote retries next pass; a failed sale keeps retrying (`sell` flag); the rest sell at pool minute 15 (`pool_age_exit`). Backtest = band-prize's 'hold to 15 while up 0%+ and rising' on the lead filter's picks. **Rolling twins `EARLY5R`/`EARLY6R`/`EARLY7R` = `early5r`/`early6r`/`early7r` (10-31, `early_rolling`, off by default, on in fly.toml):** same model and pool-minute exits; `consider_first` scores every eligible pool, keeps the past day's (seen_ts, score) in `ModelBook._history` (rebuilt from the store via `early_candidates` at start-up and set to None on an early-model reload, since a new model scores differently) and marks each signal `fixed` (score ≥ the model's cut-off, needs `early_book`) and/or `rolling` (score ≥ `rolling_cut_off` of the history, top `early_top_pct`); `early_wants(name, sig)` routes signals to `EARLY_FIXED` or `EARLY_ROLLING` books. CLI `model-status` |
| `tradingbot/risk.py` | Pure sizing + `plan_exits` (stop, time stop, trailing stop, TP ladder; moves stop to breakeven after TP1) |
| `tradingbot/engine.py` | Two threads: **monitor** (`monitor_interval_s`=10s, Jupiter `/price/v3` with DexScreener fallback, runs exits) and **scan** (`scan_interval_s`=60s: screen -> Claude -> `preflight` (fresh pair, market filters, entry drift, round trip) -> buy; then outcomes). `Book` = per-strategy cash + positions (`MAIN="claude"`); `after_screen()` hook for baselines. `preflight` takes the entry trigger price from Jupiter `/price/v3` (DexScreener lags fast movers by up to a minute). `self.lock` guards cash/position mutation; executors are called outside it. `tick()` = synchronous pass for `once`/tests |
| `tradingbot/journal.py` | Thread-safe (`@locked`, one RLock, `check_same_thread=False`). SQLite: `decisions`, `positions` (+`strategy`), `fills` (+`trigger_price` for slippage), `events` (skips, failed txs), `costs` (Claude API, per call), `equity` (snapshot per book every 60s from the monitor thread), `tracking` + `prices` (candidate price paths), `cooldowns`, `downtime`, `postmortems`, `state` (`cash:<strategy>`, `heartbeat`). `MIGRATIONS` list adds columns to older databases on start-up (e.g. `decisions.snapshot` = full pair + safety JSON). Decision stages: `screened_out`, `survivor` (passed gates, not analysed; tracked once per 6h), `analysed`. Use `query()` for read-only report SQL. `stats()` compares forward returns of Claude buys vs passes |
| `tradingbot/data/jupiter.py` | `Jupiter` client: `/swap/v1/quote` (impact is returned as a **fraction**, stored as percent), `/price/v3` (USD price, liquidity, decimals; 50 ids/call), `round_trip()` buy-then-sell quote. With `JUPITER_API_KEY` (Fly secret) calls go to `api.jup.ag` with header `x-api-key` (paid plan: 10/s, 25M requests/month), else `lite-api.jup.ag` (free: 5 per 10 s per IP). One process-wide pace `GATE` for every Jupiter call incl. `Extras.jupiter_token/_trending`: `PAID_RPS`=8/s (env `JUPITER_RPS`; 8/s for 31 days = 21.4M) or `FREE_INTERVAL_S`=2 s; hourly log line `jupiter usage: {calls, rate_limited, shared, per_hour, month_projection}`. **Shared requests (11-05):** `_get` gives identical GETs (same client, path, params) made within `SHARE_S`=1 s one call: the first goes out, the others wait on it and reuse its 200 answer (`SHARED`, counted as `shared`; failures are not reused); books buying one signal at the same $ size fill together on the same quote. Tests set `SHARE_S`=0 (conftest). A 429/5xx/timeout quote sets `last_failure()`=`RATE_LIMITED` ('Jupiter rate-limited'): the simulator reports it instead of 'no route'/'route vanished', `Engine._sell` just retries next pass (no exit_blocked, no tx_failed), `current_prices` doesn't price an emptied pool at 0 on it, model entry skips say 'Jupiter rate-limited'. Tests zero the pace (conftest autouse) |
| `tradingbot/execution/sim.py` | **Default paper executor.** Quote -> `sim_latency_s` wait -> re-quote; reverts (fee only) if below the first quote's slippage floor; fills per `sim_fill_mode`; charges priority fee + token-account rent. Exits ignore the impact cap; no route on exit = stuck position |
| `tradingbot/launch_canary.py` | **Real-money launch canary `canary_rw50_sz` (11-06; first built 11-05 as `canary_launch4_2`, then `canary_rw50_24h`; never switched on before the move)** (`model_book.CANARY4`, twin `CANARY_TWIN`=`launchrw50_sz`, `LAUNCH_CUT[CANARY4]` = the twin's flag `rw50`, thin pools only via `launch_canary_max_liq_usd`=15000 by default (fly.toml sets 0 = every pool since 11-06), sized by `band_usd` over `CANARY_SIZE_BANDS` ($5 / $0.50 / $2 below $15k / $15-25k / above, the twin's bands at 1/50) unless `launch_canary_flat_usd` > 0 (fly.toml 0.01 since 11-06: one 1-cent size for every pick), floor `engine.CANARY_MIN_USD`=0.01 (paper books keep `MODEL_MIN_USD`=5), `CANARY_BOOKS`; `launch_canary_every_n`=1 buys 1 in N of the twin's signals (state `launch_canary:count`, skip 'canary samples 1 in N signals'; the twin signals ~30 pools/h, ~0.001 SOL fees per real round trip); config `launch_canary` (needs `TB_LIVE_CONFIRM`, paper mode only) + `launch_live`; fly.toml `TB_LAUNCH_CANARY` commented out until the wallet is set up). Engine opens it only with `launch_canary_executor` (a `JupiterExecutor` built in `cli.build_engine` with the model impact cap, `launch_canary_priority_fee_sol`=0.0005, `launch_canary_sell_slippage_bps`=1000), cash `launch_canary_budget_usd`=50, fixed size `launch_canary_usd`=10 (`_buy_model` now sizes by `book.fixed_usd`); own skips: kill switch state `engine.CANARY_HALT`, `launch_canary_max_open`=5, realized loss today ≥ `launch_canary_daily_loss_usd`=50; minute-6 exit like launch4_2; `BOOK_PRIORITY` 1. Module: `status(journal, cfg)`, `calibration(journal, hours)` (pairs each canary trade with the twin's on the same token: real vs sim return, gap, swap-only gap (fill prices, before fees), entry/exit price vs sim, fee %, fill delay, sim picks not bought), `smoke(executor, mint=BONK, usd)`. CLI `canary-status [--hours N]`, `canary-stop`, `canary-start`, `canary-smoke [MINT] [--usd 2]`. Dashboard family `Canary`. Steps in DEPLOY.md |
| `tradingbot/execution/lp_pumpswap.py` | **LP route for the launch canary (11-10, real money)**: `LpPumpswapExecutor(DirectPumpswapExecutor)` (config `launch_canary_route`='lp') enters a pool as a liquidity provider instead of a buyer, because the operators pull by withdrawing their own LP (`Instruction: Withdraw`, confirmed on the 11-10 pulls), which is proportional and leaves a fellow LP's share. `buy(usd)` = one transaction: wrap SOL, buy ~half the budget of tokens (the pool's `sell`/`buy_exact_quote_in`), `DEPOSIT` them + matching SOL as liquidity (receive LP tokens); the deposit amounts are computed from reserves read at send time, `max_base`/`max_quote` cap them so a shifted pool reverts (fee only). End state: an LP position ~`usd`, half SOL half token; the atomic buy+deposit deposits ~85% of the bought tokens and leaves ~15% naked in the wallet (it can't know the exact buy output, so it deposits off the guaranteed-minimum `min_tok`). **Then `_sell_excess` (11-10) reads the real post-buy balance and SELLS the ~15% naked back to SOL in a follow-up tx** (not a deposit), so nothing is held outside the pool (user's hypothesis: operator wash-trading bots stop pumping a pool when they see an outside wallet holding its token) AND that SOL stays safe in the wallet rather than being pushed into a pool that may be drained. **(An earlier version swept the naked into the LP; the 11-10 on-chain decode showed that converts safe wallet-SOL into rug-exposed LP-SOL -- each sweep deposit spent an extra ~$1-2 of SOL that then recovered only ~50% on a rug -- so selling the excess keeps it safe instead.)** The sell (`_swap_ix(..., 'sell', naked, 1)`, min out 1, can't over-sell/revert) nets its SOL proceeds against the LP's cost; a pool already dead at this second gets ~nothing (best-effort, leaves the ~15% naked, no worse). After this the sell's `naked` term at exit is ~0, so the exit is essentially withdraw + sell the whole withdrawn share. `sell()` = **one atomic transaction** that `WITHDRAW`s our whole LP (returns our proportional share even in a drained pool) and, in the same tx, sells the whole token side back to SOL: the naked tokens the buy left (in the wallet now, read exactly) plus ~95% of the share the withdraw returns (`tok_back = naked + int(0.95*held_lp*tok_res/lp_supply)`, estimated under so it never over-sells and reverts); falls back to a withdraw-only tx when the atomic exit can't **land** -- whether it reverted on-chain OR was rejected in preflight (NOT_SENT) because the sale leg can't sell into a drained/empty pool (the common hard-rug case) -- so the SOL side is always recovered. Fills in SOL (buy usd = SOL committed, qty = LP minted; sell usd = SOL recovered). `DEPOSIT`/`WITHDRAW` discriminators, `lp_accounts` (15 accounts, no fee recipients), `lp_data`. Layouts checked against a real mainnet withdraw and a clean mainnet simulation of buy+deposit; the 11-10 $1 LP smoke landed buy+deposit (85% deposited) and the exit (withdraw+sell) on-chain. Tests `tests/test_lp_pumpswap.py` |
| `tradingbot/execution/pumpswap_direct.py` | **Direct pumpswap swaps for the launch canary (11-10, real money)**: `DirectPumpswapExecutor(JupiterExecutor)` (config `launch_canary_route`='direct', built by `cli.canary_executor`) swaps on the pool itself with no Jupiter route, trading the wallet's SOL (book still in USD at Jupiter's SOL price). Layouts from pump.fun's IDL (`pump-public-docs/idl/pump_amm.json`) checked against real mainnet swaps: v1 `buy_exact_quote_in` / `sell` + remaining accounts (buyback fee recipient from the global config, its quote-mint ATA; pump.fun coin pools put PDA('pool-v2', base_mint) first); `plan(side, token_is_base)` (canary pools have SOL as the base: buy = `sell` SOL, sale = `buy_exact_quote_in` spending tokens); tx = compute budget (`CU_LIMIT` 300k, Helius `getPriorityFeeEstimate` VeryHigh capped at `priority_fee_sol`), ATA create (wSOL, token), wrap + SyncNative, swap, close wSOL. The engine hands each signal's pool over (`remember_pool`); after a restart `_find_pool` looks it up (getProgramAccounts memcmp, most SOL). Fills from the tx: swap SOL = owner change + fee + rent of accounts the tx created; sale on an emptied pool returns 'no route (pool emptied)' unsent; min out floored at 1 (`buy_exact_quote_in` refuses 0). `GLOBAL_CONFIG`, `EVENT_AUTHORITY`, `GLOBAL_VOLUME`, `FEE_CONFIG` constants checked in tests. **Clip buys (11-11):** `launch_canary_clips` > 1 splits each buy into N equal back-to-back swaps into the same pool (a $5 buy as 5 x $1), each a distinct tx (compute-unit-price `bump` so otherwise-identical clips don't dedupe), accumulated into one Fill/position; `_buy_once` is one swap, `buy` loops it with no deliberate spacing (each confirms before the next); tests whether the running total of our capital triggers the pull or each small buy is judged on its own. **Feint probe (11-12):** `feint(token, usd)` pokes the pool with a `launch_canary_feint_usd`=$25 buy and sells it straight back, then re-reads the pool (`_state`) and returns `alive` (commit) / `pulled` (SOL vault gone or dropped ≥ `launch_canary_feint_drop_pct`=50%, skip) / `inconclusive` (couldn't place it, skip to be safe). `launch_canary_feint_hold_s`=0 (default): ONE atomic buy+sell tx (`_feint_instructions` via extracted `_swap_ix`/`_create_ata_ix`; never hold, cost ~fees, but invisible to an operator bot that watches holdings not the trade stream); > 0: buy, hold that long, then sell (visible, but a pull in the gap costs the $25). $25 is big enough to trigger the size-sensitive operators a penny probe misses, so surviving it tests the regime the real commit runs in; `launch_canary_feint_wait_s`=4 s after the poke before re-reading. Engine gate in `_buy_model` (canary book + `launch_canary_feint`, before the real `buy()`); logs event `canary_feint` {verdict, pool, sol_before, sol_after}. `RotatingExecutor.feint` probes from the wallet that will hold the commit (keeps it assigned on `alive`, frees it otherwise). `launch_canary_feint_only`=on runs the feint on every pool but never commits (measures the feint's own pull-trigger rate cheaply, no $25 at risk). Tests `tests/test_pumpswap_direct.py` (account lists pinned to two real txs; atomic round-trip has both swaps + sell min-out 1; drop detection). **1-slot probe (11-12, section 1, OFF):** `probe_1slot(token, usd)` lands committed money then pulls it out one slot later to test whether a size-reacting rugger fires the moment the commit lands (the atomic feint never lands money, so it can't trip one): free `round_trip` honeypot sim gate (a blocked exit is caught before a cent is committed), then a buy (slot N) -> `_wait_visible` (poll the buy's status and fire the sell the instant it is visible; a dependent sell can't precede the buy's slot) -> a pre-signed sell of `buy_min_out` (the guaranteed-minimum floor, dust left over) landing in the next slot, which escapes every measured pull (100% land >=2 slots after the buy); records buy/sell slots, `slot_gap`, recovered SOL and verdict ok/trapped/honeypot/buy_failed, and opens NO position (a slow-but-landed buy still sells the floor rather than leave the stake exposed). `RotatingExecutor.probe_1slot` runs it from the freshest free wallet, freeing it on a clean probe and keeping it on `trapped` (the stake is stuck there). The ~48s survival watch and the two-stage entry (survive past the per-size max pull age -> enter for real at the proper size) are sections 2-3, not built; nothing live. Tests `tests/test_rotating.py` (2) too |
| `tradingbot/execution/rotating.py` | **Wallet rotation for the launch canary (11-11, real money, OFF by default)**: `RotatingExecutor` spreads canary trades across up to `MAX_WALLETS`=10 wallets, one open trade per wallet, so each buy enters on the freshest wallet (a brand-new wallet's first trade survives; operators seem to pull once they recognise our wallet). Wraps one `DirectPumpswapExecutor` per wallet (direct route only), routes by token mint (the canary holds one position per mint): `buy` picks the least-recently-used free wallet (`_used`; none free -> `last_note` 'no free wallet (all N busy)', the engine skips), records mint->wallet in `_assign`, injects the pool, calls that sub's `buy`; a failed buy releases the wallet (keeps it fresh). `sell` routes to `_holder` (the assigned wallet, else the one whose `token_balance` is positive) and frees the wallet on a successful (full) exit. State persisted to `canary_rotation.json` next to the journal (`_assign`/`_opened`/`_used`/`pools`, reloaded on restart with pools re-applied so a trade still sells from the right wallet); a stuck rug (sale never lands) reclaims its wallet after `RECLAIM_S`=1200 s. `sweep` merges every wallet. `read_canary_wallets(cfg)` loads up to 10 keys from `launch_canary_wallets_path`, the entrypoint secret copy, or the `TB_CANARY_WALLETS` secret (JSON array of solana-keygen int-arrays or base58 strings, or base58 one-per-line/comma; deduped by pubkey). Config `launch_canary_rotate` (needs `TB_LIVE_CONFIRM`, route=direct) + `launch_canary_wallets_path`; `cli.canary_executor` builds the rotator when on + >=2 wallets (per-wallet subs' own `_pool_file` disabled so they don't clobber one shared file). Tests `tests/test_rotating.py` |
| `tradingbot/canary_slots.py` | **Slot-level timing of every real canary trade vs the operator's LP pull (11-12, read only, Helius research key)**: a Solana slot is ~400 ms and is the floor for a *dependent* sell (buy in slot N → earliest sell N+1), and block *time* is only 1-second granular, so this reads **slots**. Per canary position (reuses `canary_trades.trades`): our buy slot via `getTransaction(buy_tx)`, then the earliest liquidity withdrawal (`helius.WITHDRAW_RE`) on the pool at/after that slot whose fee payer isn't one of **our** wallets (`_our_wallets` = `read_canary_wallets` + `read_keypair`, so the LP route's own exit withdraw isn't mistaken for the rug) → `slot_gap = pull_slot - buy_slot`. Summary: median slot gap, distribution (same-slot 0 / next 1 / 2 / 3-5 / 6+), `unbeatable_le1_slot_pct` (same/next slot = no sell can escape) vs `beatable_ge2_slots_pct` (a pre-signed fast exit had a window). CLI `canary-slots [--hours N] [--max-credits 8000] [--rps 2] [--json]` (research key, never the live key). Settles whether a speed-based exit can out-run a buy-triggered rug. **By trade size (11-12):** `_by_size` groups trades by rounded cost ($25 / $5 / …) and reports per size n / pulled / rugged and the **min / median / MAX** pull time in seconds (`_pull_s` = the exact withdraw-tx gap, else the scorer's `pulled_after_fill_s`, which also catches pulls after our minute-6 exit) — the **MAX is the latest a trade of that size ever got pulled = the safe-zone edge** (survive past it + a slot or two → survivor). `rugged_no_pull` flags rugs with no pull time on record (the max could be later). Live finding (11-12): **0% of pulls are same/next-slot; 100% are ≥2 slots, 88% at 6+ slots, median gap ~18 s** — so the operator's withdraw is never bundled into our buy's block, but it's atomic, so a *reactive* sell (detect pull → sell) still can't escape (we're always one slot behind the atomic withdraw); timing makes us *safe* (a short round trip dodges all pulls) not *profitable* (one slot captures ~0 of the ~1%/min crawl, and holding long enough to beat fees re-exposes us to the rug). The lever is **selection**: the feint as a cheap (~$0.30) probe + waiting out the per-size safe-zone window, then entering confirmed survivors. Tests `tests/test_canary_slots.py` |

Table `watch_canary_path(pool, data, ts)`; export `canary_trades.json` next to the journal |

| `tradingbot/execution/jupiter.py` | **Live executor (real money), rebuilt 11-05 for the launch canary.** USDC<->token swaps via Jupiter `/swap/v1/swap`, signed locally. Fills read from the confirmed transaction (`getTransaction` jsonParsed, commitment confirmed: this wallet's pre/post token balances; fee = fee payer SOL change incl. rent, at the SOL price), so concurrent trades never mix. `rpc_url(cfg)`: Helius RPC when only the public default is set and `HELIUS_API_KEY` exists. Buy refuses before sending (`last_note`) when wallet USDC < size or SOL < `MIN_SOL`=0.01, or impact (own probe via `sim.own_impact` when Jupiter says > cap) > `max_price_impact_pct`. Send with preflight; a refusal returns a note starting `NOT_SENT`="not sent:" (engine `_sell` retries next pass instead of marking the exit blocked). Confirmation polled every `CONFIRM_POLL_S`=0.4 s up to `CONFIRM_TIMEOUT_S`=75 s, then one history lookup; dropped = failed at $0. Sells quote with `sell_slippage_bps` (`Jupiter.quote(..., slippage_bps)`); after a full sale `close_empty` closes the empty token account(s) (CloseAccount, data 9) and the returned rent counts in the proceeds. `load_keypair(text)` accepts the JSON array or a base58 private key (wallet-app export), so `TB_KEYPAIR_JSON` can hold either. Constructor takes `keypair`, `http`, `sleep` for tests (`tests/test_launch_canary.py`) |

Token missing from both feeds while others quote = 0 (rug); no prices at all = sample skipped |

Served by the health server on 8080 (fly.toml `[http_service]`, public HTTPS at `https://supermeme.fly.dev/`) only when the Fly secret `DASHBOARD_TOKEN` is set (else 404; `/health` always open). Auth: `?key=<token>` once -> 303 + HttpOnly cookie `tb_dash` (1 year), compared with `hmac.compare_digest`. Routes `/` (one HTML page `PAGE`), `/api/summary` (JSON), `/api/stream` (server-sent events). **Since 11-05 the page computes every table itself** from raw rows: one shared `Live` poller (thread `dashboard-live`, every `POLL_S`=2 s) reads on its own read-only sqlite connections (journal `file:...?mode=ro`, watch.db likewise; never the journal lock): positions of `model_book.active_books` (`POS_COLS` + `blocked_since` from `exit_blocked` events, scanned incrementally by event id), fills (`fills_after`, feed of the last 300), `watch_launch_live` rows of the last `LAUNCH_KEEP_S`=24 h (`LAUNCH_COLS` + flags from `launch_live.low_flags`/`rw_flags`), health every 10 s; each changed row gets a version, streams send `init` (all rows, books with hold and `LAUNCH_CUT` flag, cash, stuck seconds), then `delta` (rows newer than the page's version) and `fill`. Browser: equity = cash + closed (realized - cost) + open at cost, $0 after `STUCK_AFTER_S`=1800 s blocked; return, max drawdown on closed trades, win, median/mean, PF, rugged (≤ -80%); launch window 1/6/24 h: ideal outcomes per book flag (rugged = ret ≤ -99.9%, +50% wins) and real vs ideal (closed trades matched by token, missed = flagged pools the book never held). `summary` (backtest notes `EXPECTED`, downtime, last backup) every `SUMMARY_S`=300 s from a niced child (`python -m tradingbot dashboard-summary PATH`). Numbers can differ slightly from `report` (rug definition, stuck timing). **Views (11-05, hash router, tabs):** `#/` overview, `#/ledger` trade ledger (every position of the running books, newest first; status Open / Exit blocked / Stuck ($0) / Won / Lost, result %, result $, held, cost, exit reason; filters family / book / status / token; 200 rows + 'show more'; token links to DexScreener), `#/books` family cards, `#/family/<name>` (tiles, books table, equity lines per book, family ledger), `#/book/<name>` (tiles, equity curve, real vs ideal in the launch window, book ledger). Families: `dashboard.FAMILIES` / `family_of(name)` (Launch picks, Low decile, Rug-vs-win, Early, model5), sent in `init` as `books[].family` + `families`. Equity charts are inline SVG (ink tones + dash patterns, never lime lines). Hidden views need `[hidden]{display:none!important}` (the `main` grid display overrode it once). **Programs (11-05):** top tabs `#/` MZFq (renamed from All programs 11-05; combined P&L tiles; a card per program: Supermeme = every book with its families table, MZF Short Only = NAV, return, open/closed shorts, drawdown, Strategy 1 groups vs research) / `#/supermeme` (the old overview; sub-tabs Overview, Trade ledger, Books & families shown only under Supermeme) / `#/shorts` (MZF Short Only: tiles, Strategy 1 groups vs research, NAV chart, open shorts, closed shorts, recent listings). **Market (11-07):** Supermeme sub-tab `#/market`: shift count + key tiles (liquid pools/h, thin share, rug %, market ideal mean, rug-vs-win gap, sz real, each with the past-week value and z), 'moved away from the past week' (|z| ≥ 2, sorted), six hourly charts with the weekly mean dashed (`chart(..., empty)`), every measure grouped, and the last 48 hours; note when the monitor is off, has no hours yet, or its newest hour is over ~3 h old. The short book is another Fly app: `Dashboard.fetch_shorts` (thread `dashboard-shorts`, every `SHORTS_POLL_S`=30 s, `SHORTS_TIMEOUT_S`=8) GETs `cfg.shortbook_feed_url` (fly.toml `TB_SHORTBOOK_FEED_URL=http://shortbook-paper.internal:8080/api/feed`, Fly private network); a failure keeps the last data with `stale`/`error`; streams send event `shorts`; init carries `shorts_enabled`. Ships as `vN` and calls `restart`. `review()` probation: rollback to parent on ≥`code_max_errors`=20 `loop_error` events, on 0 analyses with ≥5 survivors after 12h, or when edge vs `filters_only` over `code_probation_trades`=10 drops > `code_rollback_margin_pct`=5 vs before.
