- **2026-10-28 (rug-aware labels, fixed):** `watch_analysis.label(path, start, entry, pool_snaps, entry_liq, watched_after)` is now the one labeller (build_dataset + `model_book.training_rows`): `rug_ts` = first later snapshot whose liquidity is < `RUG_LIQ_SHARE`=10% of entry; if nobody traded the token afterwards on any pool (graduations keep trading on the new pool), `_outcome(..., pulled_ts)` keeps a TP/SL that happened before it and otherwise scores -100% (`trade_ret` -1.0, `worst_mult` 0); no trades at all -> `RUG_OUTCOME` instead of `FLAT_OUTCOME`. New label key `rugged` (in `LABEL_KEYS`, never a feature). On the 10-26 backup (8 days of model5 training rows): 17% of all rows and **53% of rows with ≥$5k liquidity at 5 min** are rugged; the collapse is real (4 of 3,801 recovered), first seen at 15 min 984, 30 min 1,023, 60 min 830, 3h 602, 6h 217, 24h 145; ≥$5k rows average -42.5%/trade. Training target is still `pump2x`. Next: walk-forward of model5 on the corrected labels (current model vs rug-aware target/filter).
- **2026-10-28 (LABEL BUG found):** on the 10-26 backup, 246 of 2,155 top-10% picks (≥$5k liq) got `FLAT_OUTCOME` (-6%, 'watched but never traded again'); 240 of them had liquidity pulled to ~$0 within 10-15 min (later snapshots liq <10% of entry), i.e. -100% rugs. Counting FLAT picks as -100%: model5-style walk-forward picks (top 2%, ≥$5k) go from +20.6% to **+4.8%/trade** (median +14%, rug rate 28.4%; by day +21.9, +8.1, -0.7, -6.2, +10.1, +0.4, -4.1%). With corrected labels a rug filter matters: dropping the 25% most rug-like picks (snapshot-only logistic on 'pulled') -> +15.5%/trade, total $4,115 vs $1,709 per $100 trades (356 picks, 7 days: noisy). Live: 5 of 8 model5 buys rugged. Next (proposed to the user): fix `FLAT_OUTCOME`/`_outcome` so a later liquidity collapse (<10% of entry) before the exit = -100% (watch_analysis + model_book.training_rows; non-FLAT rows that rug after some trading are also mislabelled now), then retrain/evaluate model5 with a rug-aware target or filter. Scripts: scratchpad flat.py, rug6.py, rug7.py.
- **2026-10-28 (frozen prices):** report at 10-27 20:01 UTC: model5 +1.97% ($10,196.64), but its three open positions (NEX Ai, 姆巴佩·毛泽东 Cn2Sf…, MOGZ) were all rugged: Jupiter `/price/v3` no longer listed them, DexScreener kept the last trade price with liquidity $0, and a sell quote had no route. They were valued at ~$428 from those frozen prices (real equity ~$9,768, about -2.3%) and their stops never fired. `Engine.current_prices` now drops DexScreener prices with liquidity below `DEAD_LIQ_USD`=100 (None, as on bonding curves, is kept); held dead tokens are priced by `sell_price`, and at 0.0 when there is no route, so the stop fires, the exit is blocked and the position is valued at $0 after `STUCK_AFTER_S`. So far 5 of 8 model5 buys rugged (\*\*, DICTATOR + these three) vs 11.5% in the research. Report caveat: model5's median exit slippage (9.14%) includes the \*\* fill against its bogus 0.5227 trigger; the genuine exits were -1.1%, -3.1% and -15.2%. 240 tests.
- **2026-10-28 (rug logging):** user asked to log rugs live instead of filtering. After each model5 buy, `Engine._log_model_audit` stores Jupiter's holder audit (`model_book.entry_audit` via `Extras.jupiter_token`: holders, top-holder %, dev balance %, dev mints/migrations, organic score, liquidity, `is_sus`, authorities) + score and snapshot liquidity as event `model_entry_audit`. On a losing close (`_sell`) or the first exit block (`_exit_blocked`), `_log_exit_cause` stores `model_exit_cause` once per position: `no_route`, `liquidity_pulled` (Jupiter liquidity now < `LIQ_PULLED_SHARE`=25% of entry), `dumped` (≤ `RUG_RETURN`=-80% with the pool intact) or `stopped`. `model-status` gains `rugs` (`rug_summary`: live rug rate vs research 11.5%, causes, median entry audit of rugs vs others, % flagged suspicious). `Engine(extras=)`, built in `cli.build_engine` when `model_book`. All fail soft. Live check: MOGZ (open) showed Jupiter liquidity ~$0, dev balance 86%, `isSus` true. Positions bought before this deploy have no audit. 239 tests.
- **2026-10-28 (rug analysis, no code change):** on the 10-26 backup, "rug" = simulated trade ≤ -80% (the -50% stop gapped to ≤0.26x). ≥$5k-liquidity rows: 15.5% rug (pumpswap 22%, meteora-damm-v2 5%); walk-forward model5-style picks (top 2%, ≥$5k): 11.5% rug, +20.6%/trade. Rugs are predictable from the 5-min snapshot (held-out AUC 0.83) but by the same features that predict pumps: a walk-forward rug filter dropping the riskiest 25% of picks cut rugs to 7.5% and raised the mean to +26.7%/trade, yet total profit was flat ($7,349 vs $7,094 per $100 trades). Jupiter holder audit (holders, top-holder %, dev mints/migrations, organic score; fetched at ~15 min, so only testable at the 15-min age) added nothing (AUC 0.829 -> 0.830). Research fills a gapped stop at the candle close; live no-route rugs are -100%, so the live rug cost can be worse than modelled. Scripts were in the session scratchpad (rug1-5.py), not in the repo.
- **2026-10-28 (bogus take-profit):** a model5 position ("**", bought 2.49e-05) was sold as `take_profit_1` with trigger 0.5227 (21,000x entry) and got ~$0: the pool had been drained, no feed listed the token, and `quote_price` (a $20 *buy* quote) on an empty pool implies a huge price. The loss was real (rug); the label and the equity mark were wrong. Now `Engine.current_prices(tokens, held)` prices unlisted held tokens with `Jupiter.sell_price(mint, qty)` (quote to sell the position), and `manage_positions` confirms every take-profit with a sell quote (`_confirm_sell_price`; if lower, the exits are re-planned at that price, so a rug exits as `stop_loss`). Side effect: a take-profit now needs the sell quote for the whole position (incl. impact) to clear +100%. 姆巴佩·毛泽东 was bought twice (12:07, 12:49): verified as two different mints with the same name (Cn2Sf… still open, ERhUg… took profit), so no duplicate-buy bug. Position 227 keeps its old `take_profit_1` label in the journal. 236 tests.

Known, not fixed: equity snapshots taken before 10-28 valued DICTATOR at its last price (drawdown understated for that day); exit slippage is measured vs the trigger price, which overshoots the target on spikes. 234 tests.

Now `Engine._sell` treats a failed fill with usd 0 (nothing sent) as `_exit_blocked`: retry every `EXIT_RETRY_S`=120, one `exit_blocked` event per hour (blocked-since restored from events after restarts), valued at $0 in equity after `STUCK_AFTER_S`=1800 (`_position_value`). - **2026-10-11 (api errors):** billing/auth/transient Claude errors pause analysis instead of setting candidates aside. 65 tests. Needs `fly deploy` to reach the running bot.
- **2026-10-11 (deploy):** Fly.io deployment, watchdog + `/health`, `health`/`backup` commands. Image built and run in Docker here: health OK, report/backup via exec, key kept off the volume. Not yet deployed to Fly by anyone. 60 tests.

## Realism roadmap

1. Done: quote-based fills, round-trip check, fresh price at buy.
2. Done: separate fast position-monitor thread (Jupiter price API) + trigger price on every fill.
3. Done: Claude/tx cost accounting + richer report (equity curve, drawdown, medians, CI).
4. Done: shadow portfolios (`filters_only`, `random`); report prints Claude's edge over each after API cost.
5. Done: persisted cooldowns + analyst hourly budget (seeded from `costs`), heartbeat-based downtime log, tracker-driven 1h/6h/24h outcomes with max up/down for bought, passed and not-analysed candidates.
6. Done: canary live trades for calibration + replay tool from recorded price paths.

All six parts are complete. Next real-world step: run in paper mode on an always-on machine with an API key for 2+ weeks, then read `report`.
